TL;DR: 本文提出TRAIL方法，利用冻结的DINO编码器的patch-level扰动响应进行训练无关的图像编辑定位，仅需一次全局Haar扰动并计算patch token间的余弦漂移即可生成定位图，无需训练或掩码监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部图像编辑可以改变照片含义而大部分区域保持真实，因此取证分析必须定位编辑发生的位置。现有方法通常需要掩码监督训练，缺乏训练无关的通用定位方案。

Method: TRAIL方法：对输入图像施加一次全局Haar扰动，利用冻结的DINO ViT编码器提取patch token，计算扰动前后对应patch token之间的余弦漂移，该漂移本身即为局部编辑的定位图。选择位于归一化深度0.80-0.94的编码器层块以获得最佳定位信号。

Result: 在80张源不相交的CocoGlide测试图像上，TRAIL达到0.903的patch AUROC（对比掩码监督的Detective SAM的0.912）；固定阈值Dice为0.619（对比0.709），而oracle阈值可将TRAIL提升至0.790。迁移到Poisson图像插值任务上达到0.855 AUROC（对比0.864），证明该信号无需生成器即可存在。AUROC从全局扰动的0.903降至局部扰动的0.857和独立裁剪编码的0.735，表明全局上下文对信号可见性至关重要。

Conclusion: 冻结DINO的patch token在深层包含强定位信号，其可见性同时依赖于扰动的全局性和保留的上下文信息。该方法为训练无关的AI图像编辑取证定位提供了有效且可迁移的解决方案。

Abstract: Localized image edits can change a photograph's meaning while leaving most of it authentic, so forensic analysis must identify where an edit occurred. We show that patch-level perturbation responses from frozen DINO encoders are themselves localization maps. Training-free Localization of AI-image Edits from patch-token Drift (TRAIL) applies one global Haar perturbation and maps cosine drift between corresponding patch tokens. On 80 source-disjoint CocoGlide test images, TRAIL reaches .903 patch AUROC versus .912 for the mask-supervised Detective SAM; fixed-threshold Dice is .619 versus .709, while an oracle threshold raises TRAIL to .790. Transferred unchanged to Poisson image interpolation, TRAIL reaches .855 AUROC versus .864, showing that the cue persists without a generator. Across sixteen DINO encoders, the best block lies at normalized depth .80-.94. Global context matters: AUROC falls from .903 globally to .857 for local-in-canvas perturbations and .735 for independently encoded crops. Frozen DINO patch tokens therefore contain a strong late-layer localization signal whose visibility depends on the perturbation and preserved context. Code: https://github.com/VishalJ99/trail-image-edit-localization.

</details>


### [66] [When Simplicity Wins: Bottleneck-Aware Context Modeling for Lightweight Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18979)
*Mian Muhammad Naeem Abid,Nancy Mehta,Zongwei Wu,Radu Timofte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SiConMo，一个轻量级语义分割框架，核心设计原则是：在极低计算预算下，瓶颈阶段是整合局部与全局上下文信息最高效的环节。包含RGB-only和GME增强两个变体，在ADE20K、PASCAL Context、Cityscapes和COCO-Stuff上实现了最先进的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义分割需要在精度、效率和可扩展性之间取得平衡，尤其对高分辨率图像而言。卷积网络擅长局部模式但缺乏长程依赖建模能力，ViT能捕获全局上下文但计算成本过高。现有研究大多聚焦于编码器设计，而瓶颈阶段——对上下文聚合和信息流动至关重要的环节——被相对忽视。

Method: SiConMo由三个互补组件构成：(1) Token金字塔提取模块，用于分层多尺度表示；(2) Transformer分支深度可分离卷积块，用于瓶颈感知的上下文建模；(3) 特征合并模块，在保持空间结构的同时增强语义一致性。框架分为RGB-only版本（SiConMo）和GME增强版本（SiConMo†）两个变体。

Result: 在ADE20K、PASCAL Context、Cityscapes和COCO-Stuff四个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，SiConMo在轻量级语义分割模型中取得了最先进的精度-效率权衡，验证了简洁性作为一种强大设计原则的有效性。

Conclusion: 文章的核心发现是"简单性"的力量：在极低计算预算下，将局部与全局上下文的整合放在瓶颈阶段而非编码器阶段是最高效的选择。这一原则使得SiConMo在保持轻量化的同时达到了优异的精度-效率平衡。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation demands a careful balance between accuracy, efficiency, and scalability, which remains difficult to achieve for high-resolution imagery. Convolutional networks effectively model local patterns but struggle with long-range dependencies, whereas Vision Transformers capture global context at a high computational cost. While recent work largely focuses on encoder design, the bottleneck stage, central to contextual aggregation and information flow, has been relatively overlooked. We propose SiConMo, a lightweight yet effective framework, implemented in two variants: an RGB-only model (SiConMo) and a GME-enhanced variant (SiConMo$_\dagger$). We show that simplicity arises from a key design principle: at very low computational budgets, the bottleneck is the most efficient stage to integrate local and global context. SiConMo integrates three complementary components: a Token Pyramid Extraction Module for hierarchical multi-scale representation, a Transformer-Branched Depthwise Convolution block for bottleneck-aware context modeling, and a Feature Merging Module that preserves spatial structure while enhancing semantic consistency. Extensive experiments on ADE20K, PASCAL Context, Cityscapes, and COCO-Stuff demonstrate that SiConMo achieves a state-of-the-art accuracy-efficiency trade-off among lightweight semantic segmentation models, highlighting simplicity as a powerful design principle.

</details>


### [67] [Uncertainty-Aware Art-Historical Dating with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18984)
*Stefanie Schneider,Peter Bell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 博物馆和档案数据集并非历史艺术生产的镜像，而是收藏、保存、编目和数字化等偶然历史的物化。预训练图像表征可能看似编码了历史时间，实则编码了机构条件下物体如何成为可见数据。本文将这一现象定义为"时间纠缠"，并通过不确定感知的回归任务在冻结图像嵌入上进行艺术品年代推断研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉模型在艺术品数据上表现出的时间编码能力可能并非真实反映历史时间，而是反映了数据集构建过程中的机构偏差（如收藏偏好、保存历史、编目方式、数字化质量等）。理解这一"时间纠缠"现象对于可信地使用预训练表征进行历史研究至关重要。

Method: 将艺术品年代推断构建为不确定感知（uncertainty-aware）的回归任务，使用冻结的图像嵌入（frozen image embeddings），在按时间控制的Wikidata艺术品语料库上评估多种预训练视觉模型，包括纯视觉自监督模型和视觉-语言模型（VLMs），并进行定性分析揭示时间知识中的偏差来源。

Result: 预训练模型包含可用的时间信息，其中视觉-语言模型（VLMs）优于纯视觉自监督基线。但定性分析表明，这些模型的时间知识受到各种偏差的影响，体现了"时间纠缠"现象。

Conclusion: 预训练图像表征中的时间信号并非纯粹反映历史创作时间，而可能与机构性数据形成条件深度纠缠。这提醒研究者在将预训练模型用于历史性视觉任务时，需警惕数据集中隐藏的制度性偏差。

Abstract: Museum and archival datasets do not mirror historical artistic production, but materialize the contingent histories of collecting, preservation, cataloging, and digitization. This has direct consequences for interpreting pretrained image representations: they may appear to encode historical time while actually encoding the institutional conditions under which objects become visible as data. We describe this phenomenon as temporal entanglement and investigate it by formulating artwork dating as an uncertainty-aware regression task over frozen image embeddings. We evaluate several pretrained vision models on a temporally controlled Wikidata corpus of artworks. Our results show that these models contain usable temporal information, with Vision-Language Models (VLMs) outperforming purely visual self-supervised baselines. However, a qualitative analysis indicates that this temporal knowledge is shaped by various biases.

</details>


### [68] [X-LMC: Cross-View Spatiotemporal Collateral Circulation Scoring from DSA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18986)
*Maedeh Hafezi Moghadas,Hakim Baazaoui,Lukas Bastian Otto,Susanne Wegener,Björn Menze,Ezequiel De la Rosa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出X-LMC，一个基于时空深度学习框架的软脑膜侧支（LMC）自动评分系统，从时间分辨的双平面数字减影血管造影（DSA）中自动评估ASITN/SIR分级，在多中心134例M1段闭塞患者数据集上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSA是LMC评估的金标准，但临床上的ASITN/SIR分级依赖人工视觉检查，主观性强、变异大，缺乏自动化、客观的评分工具。

Method: X-LMC架构包含三个核心组件：①DINOv2骨干网络编码空间帧表示；②token级跨视角注意力模块融合双平面（正交投影）信息；③循环神经网络建模对比剂团注的动态时间动力学。

Result: 在多中心134例M1段闭塞患者数据上，5折交叉验证中X-LMC取得QWK 0.398（基线最佳0.322）和二分宏观F1 0.711（基线最佳0.663），性能与临床观察者间一致性（QWK 0.314）相当。

Conclusion: 作为首个自动化LMC评分的DSA研究，X-LMC证明多视角时序深度学习可捕捉侧支特异性的对比剂动力学特征，为客观血流动力学表型分型建立了可复现的基础，同时揭示了临床分级的模糊性和自动化ASITN/SIR评分的性能边界。

Abstract: Digital subtraction angiography (DSA) is the reference standard for leptomeningeal collateral (LMC) assessment, providing critical prognostic insights to guide secondary treatment strategies, neurorehabilitation planning, and retrospective stroke research. However, clinical LMC grading via the ASITN/SIR scale relies on manual, highly variable visual inspection. We introduce X-LMC, a spatiotemporal framework for automated collateral scoring from time-resolved biplane DSA. The proposed architecture encodes spatial frame representations through a DINOv2 backbone, fuses orthogonal projections via a token-level cross-view attention module, and models representations of contrast bolus dynamics using a recurrent network architecture. We evaluate our framework on a multicenter dataset of 134 patients with M1-segment occlusions. In a 5-fold cross-validation setting, X-LMC yields higher point estimates than static architectures and spatiotemporal baselines adapted from related angiographic tasks, achieving a Quadratic Weighted Kappa (QWK) of 0.398 (vs. 0.322) and a dichotomized macro-F1 score of 0.711 (vs. 0.663) against the best-performing baseline. X-LMC performance also aligns with the observed clinical inter-rater agreement (QWK: 0.314). As the first DSA study attempting to automate LMC scoring, we demonstrate that multi-view temporal deep learning can capture collateral-specific contrast kinetics. Ultimately, these benchmarks delineate the clinical ambiguities and achievable performance boundaries of automated ASITN/SIR grading, establishing a reproducible foundation for objective hemodynamic phenotyping in stroke cohorts. Code is available at https://github.com/maedehafezi/X-LMC.

</details>


### [69] [ForeSightGuide: An Anticipatory Framework toward Accurate and Low-Redundancy Guidance for the Visually Impaired](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18993)
*Zhiyuan Wang,Xu Li,Shikang Guo,Wei Meng,Quan Liu,Jie Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ForeSightGuide是一个基于视觉语言模型的前瞻性辅助导航框架，通过预测障碍物运动而非仅做反应式感知，有效过滤非威胁物体，减少视障人士在动态场景中的认知过载。在公共基准和新提出的真实交通场景数据集上达到SOTA性能，将冗余警报降至0.299/次同时保持低漏报率0.112。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的电子出行辅助系统虽借助VLM提供丰富的环境理解能力，但在动态场景中容易产生大量误报（false positives），对视障用户造成认知过载，影响独立出行的安全性和有效性。需要一种能够预测而非仅反应的环境感知方案。

Method: 提出ForeSightGuide框架，将语义场景理解与预测性危险评估相结合。利用VLM的推理能力预测障碍物的运动趋势，主动过滤非威胁物体，仅输出简洁、可执行的导航指令。同时构建了一个全新的、涵盖复杂动态真实交通场景的数据集用于评测预测能力。

Result: 在公共基准和新提出的数据集上均取得最优性能（SOTA）。冗余警报率降至0.299/次指导输出，漏报危险率低至0.112，在减少信息过载的同时保持较高的安全性。

Conclusion: ForeSightGuide证明了前瞻性预测框架比传统反应式系统更适用于视障人士的行走辅助，能有效平衡信息精简与安全保障，为可穿戴电子出行辅助设备的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Electronic travel aids are pivotal for the independent mobility of the visually impaired. While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) offer rich environmental understanding, they often suffer from excessive false positives in dynamic scenarios, leading to cognitive overload. To address this, we present ForeSightGuide, an anticipatory assistive guidance framework that couples semantic scene understanding with predictive hazard assessment. Unlike reactive systems, ForeSightGuide leverages the reasoning capabilities of VLMs to anticipate obstacle motion, effectively filtering out non-threatening objects to provide concise, actionable guidance. To validate our approach, we introduce a novel dataset captured in complex, dynamic real-world traffic scenes, designed to benchmark predictive capabilities. Extensive experiments on both public benchmarks and our proposed dataset demonstrate that ForeSightGuide achieves state-of-the-art performance. Notably, it significantly mitigates information overload by reducing redundant alerts to 0.299 per guidance output while maintaining a low missed-hazard rate of 0.112, proving its efficacy for safe walking assistance.

</details>


### [70] [GrabVG: Graph-Attentive Binding for Visual Grounding in UAV Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18996)
*Chaowei Wang,Yan Di,Jingjun Sun,Baozhe Liu,Jiaxu Tian,Yuheng Li,Guangqian Guo,Shan Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GrabVG，一种受人类视觉搜索启发的无人机图像视觉定位框架，通过预注意假设搜索和图注意特征绑定两阶段策略，解决高拥挤场景下小目标视觉冗余和拓扑模糊问题，在AerialVG和AerialSense数据集上分别达到67.31%和80.34%的Acc@0.5，超越基线10.55和8.76个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机图像中的视觉定位面临两大核心挑战：(1) 大量小而密集且视觉相似的目标造成高视觉冗余，使现有方法难以区分细微的实例间差异；(2) 重复的局部配置引发强拓扑模糊性，现有方法主要依赖视觉-语言特征对齐或密集上下文交互，无法有效利用空间拓扑结构，导致在高度拥挤场景下定位不准确。

Method: GrabVG将视觉定位显式分解为两个顺序阶段：(1) 预注意假设搜索（Preattentive Hypothesis Search）：通过蒸馏引导的提议归纳和文本感知假设过滤，生成紧凑可靠的目标假设集合，减少背景干扰和语义不匹配；(2) 图注意特征绑定（Graph-Attentive Feature Binding）：将假设组织为稀疏图，通过图注意力将语言引导的实例内视觉线索与实例间拓扑关系联合绑定与传播，实现高效空间推理和精确目标定位。

Result: 在AerialVG和AerialSense两个数据集上，GrabVG分别达到67.31%和80.34%的Acc@0.5，相对于对应基线分别提升10.55和8.76个百分点，同时实现了良好的精度-速度权衡。

Conclusion: GrabVG通过模拟人类视觉搜索机制，将视觉定位分解为预注意假设搜索和图注意特征绑定两个阶段，有效解决了无人机高拥挤场景下的视觉冗余和拓扑模糊问题，在精度和效率之间取得了优越的平衡。

Abstract: Visual grounding in Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) imagery aims to localize a target object in complex bird's-eye-view scenes according to a natural language description. However, the abundance of small, densely distributed, and visually similar objects creates high visual redundancy, while repetitive local configurations give rise to strong topological ambiguity. Existing approaches mainly focus on visual--language feature alignment or dense contextual interaction, yet they struggle to distinguish subtle inter-instance differences and effectively exploit spatial topological structures, leading to inaccurate grounding in highly crowded scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose $\textbf{GrabVG}$, a novel visual grounding framework inspired by human visual search. GrabVG explicitly decomposes grounding into two sequential stages: $\textit{preattentive hypothesis search}$ and $\textit{graph-attentive feature binding}$. Specifically, we first generate a compact set of reliable object hypotheses through distillation-guided proposal induction and text-aware hypothesis filtering, substantially reducing background distractions and semantic mismatches. These hypotheses are then organized into a sparse graph, where language-guided intra-instance visual cues and inter-instance topological relationships are jointly bound and propagated via graph attention, enabling efficient spatial reasoning and accurate target localization. Extensive experiments on AerialVG and AerialSense show that GrabVG achieves a favorable accuracy--speed trade-off, reaching 67.31$\%$ and 80.34$\%$ Acc@0.5 and outperforming the corresponding baselines by 10.55 and 8.76 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [71] [Mise-en-Scène: Implicit Layout Emergence in Diffusion Transformers for Human-AI Design Co-Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19000)
*Zipeng Xu,Ryan Murdock,Umberto Michieli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Mise-en-Scène，一种两阶段框架，利用预训练的图像编辑扩散Transformer隐式生成版式，再通过确定性匹配-放置步骤将原始高分辨率素材放置到设计位置，在PrismLayersPlus基准上感知质量大幅超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将空间规划与视觉合成分离，先用语言模型预测显式边界框坐标然后粘贴素材，导致构图僵硬、缩放失当。作者提出质疑：版式能否在预训练图像编辑扩散Transformer内部隐式涌现，从而使元素排列与画布渲染联合生成。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 第一阶段：扩散Transformer通过小型、经过knockout选择的LoRA进行适配，草拟完整设计，元素的排列与渲染画布联合涌现；(2) 第二阶段：确定性的匹配-放置步骤将原始高分辨率素材移动到草拟位置，确保素材保真度并生成可编辑的分层设计。该方法仅需对预训练Transformer进行最小适配，无需多元素生成常用的专门条件机制。

Result: 在大规模PrismLayersPlus基准上，Mise-en-Scène生成的设计在感知质量上最接近真实值，以显著优势超越LLM版式规划器和专用版式Transformer；匹配-放置阶段弥补了与真实值合成之间剩余的画面保真度差距。

Conclusion: 该研究表明版式可以在扩散Transformer中隐式涌现，无需显式的边界框预测机制，且通过后续的匹配-放置步骤在保证素材保真度的同时，生成设计师可继续编辑的分层设计，而非扁平图像。

Abstract: Automating graphic design synthesis from user-provided elements requires both a coherent overall composition and the exact preservation of each asset. Existing methods predict a layout as explicit bounding-box coordinates with a language model and then paste the assets into it, which separates spatial planning from visual synthesis and tends to produce rigid, mis-scaled compositions. We instead ask whether the layout can emerge implicitly inside a pretrained image-editing diffusion transformer. We present Mise-en-Scène, a two-stage framework. In the first stage, a diffusion transformer adapted with a small, knockout-selected LoRA drafts a complete design in which the arrangement of the elements emerges jointly with the rendered canvas. In the second stage, a deterministic match-and-place step moves the original high-resolution assets to the drafted positions, which guarantees exact asset fidelity and yields an editable, layered design that a designer can keep refining rather than a flat image. Notably, a minimal adaptation of the pretrained transformer already suffices, without the specialized conditioning machinery commonly introduced for multi-element generation. On the large-scale PrismLayersPlus benchmark, the designs produced by Mise-en-Scène are the closest to the ground truth in perceived quality among all compared methods, by a wide margin over both an LLM layout planner and a specialized layout transformer, while our match-and-place stage bridges the remaining fidelity gap to the ground-truth composites.

</details>


### [72] [One-Stage Object Detectors in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19014)
*Jonel Roman,Ryan Sirjue,Peter Nguyen,Daniel Krutky,Juan Jesus,Sudip Dhakal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于自动驾驶中单阶段目标检测器的综合性综述，系统梳理了从YOLOv1、SSD、RetinaNet到YOLOv10等主流架构的设计演进，并从设计选择、特征融合、损失函数、部署权衡和基准性能等多个维度进行了对比分析，同时总结了相关数据集、评估指标、开放挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要快速可靠的实时感知系统来检测车辆、行人、骑行者、交通标志等道路目标。单阶段目标检测器在速度与精度之间提供了关键平衡，已成为自动驾驶感知的重要技术路线。然而该领域发展迅速、架构繁多，缺乏系统性的横向对比与综合分析，因此需要通过综述来梳理技术演进脉络并识别现存差距。

Method: 本文采用综述研究方法，而非提出新的检测系统。具体包括：(1)回顾单阶段检测器的演进历程，涵盖YOLOv1、SSD、RetinaNet、EfficientDet、anchor-free检测器（FCOS、CenterNet）及YOLOv10等近期实时模型；(2)从设计选择、特征融合策略、损失函数、部署权衡和基准性能等维度进行架构对比；(3)总结常用自动驾驶数据集、评估指标、开放挑战及未来研究方向。

Result: 综述揭示了单阶段检测器在速度、精度、效率与鲁棒性之间平衡的不同设计策略，并明确指出基准测试结果与实际自动驾驶部署性能之间仍存在显著差距，该差距是当前领域面临的核心问题。

Conclusion: 单阶段检测器为自动驾驶感知提供了高效的解决方案，但现有模型在真实复杂道路环境中的可靠性仍不足以完全满足自动驾驶的高安全性要求，未来需要在提升鲁棒性、缩小基准与实车部署差距方面持续探索。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles depend on fast and reliable perception systems to detect surrounding vehicles, pedestrians, cyclists, traffic signs, and other road objects in real time. This paper presents a comprehensive survey and analysis of one-stage object detectors for autonomous driving rather than an implementation of a new detection system. The survey reviews the evolution of major one-stage detectors, including YOLOv1, SSD, RetinaNet, EfficientDet, anchor-free detectors such as FCOS and CenterNet, and recent real-time models such as YOLOv10. It compares these architectures through their design choices, feature-fusion strategies, loss functions, deployment trade-offs, and reported benchmark performance. The paper also summarizes commonly used autonomous-driving datasets, evaluation metrics, open challenges, and future research directions. Overall, this survey highlights how one-stage detectors balance speed, accuracy, efficiency, and robustness, while also emphasizing the remaining gap between benchmark results and dependable real-world autonomous-driving performance.

</details>


### [73] [Orthogonal Polynomial Approximation for Matrix Log Normalization in Global Covariance Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19021)
*Md Rifat Ur Rahman,Md Raihan Khan,Md Sakib Hossain Shovon,Pietro Liò,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需特征分解的矩阵对数归一化方法用于全局协方差池化，通过均值特征值预归一化和多项式近似（推荐8阶Chebyshev展开）替代不稳定的谱方法，在多个基准上比谱对数及平方根近似更快更准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局协方差池化需要SPD流形上的归一化步骤，矩阵对数是最忠实的选择，但其基于特征分解的梯度数值不稳定，导致被平方根方法取代。作者发现这种不稳定性源于谱计算方式而非对数本身，因此希望设计一种无特征分解、数值稳定的对数近似方法。

Method: 用有限多项式（推荐8阶Chebyshev展开，通过三项矩阵递推计算）近似协方差矩阵的对数；关键贡献是均值特征值预归一化将谱中心化到接近1处（远离log的奇点），并用标量后补偿在闭式形式中恢复log(A)的奇异部分。前向和反向传播均只涉及广义矩阵乘法（GEMM），梯度在预归一化协方差的谱支撑上有界，且不出现1/(lambda_i-lambda_j)不稳定项。同时研究了Legendre、Laguerre、Taylor和Pade展开作为对照。

Result: 在三个细粒度基准和ImageNet-1k上，无特征分解的对数方法比谱对数及平方根近似更快且更准确；在匹配基数和阶数下，对数目标优于平方根目标，证明性能提升源于忠实的Riemannian映射而非更好的多项式族。

Conclusion: 矩阵对数的不稳定性是谱计算方式的人为产物，通过多项式近似配合均值特征值预归一化可以获得既数值稳定又高效的GCP归一化方法，且验证了对数作为Riemannian流形映射的优越性。

Abstract: Global Covariance Pooling (GCP) improves deep networks by capturing second-order feature statistics, and is especially effective for fine-grained recognition. Because covariance matrices live on the Symmetric Positive Definite (SPD) manifold, a normalization step is required before the Euclidean classifier. The faithful choice is the matrix logarithm (MLN-COV), which maps the SPD manifold to its tangent space; in practice it was abandoned in favour of the matrix square root because its eigendecomposition-based gradient is numerically unstable. We show that this instability is an artifact of computing the logarithm spectrally, not of the logarithm itself. Approximating the logarithm with finite polynomials in the covariance matrix removes the eigendecomposition from both passes: every operation becomes a General Matrix Multiplication (GEMM), the gradient stays bounded on the spectral support of the pre-normalized covariance, and the unstable 1/(lambda_i-lambda_j) term never appears. The key ingredient is a mean-eigenvalue pre-normalization that centres the spectrum near 1, away from the singularity of log, with a scalar post-compensation that returns the singular part of log(A) in closed form. Our recommended normalizer is a degree-8 Chebyshev expansion evaluated by a three-term matrix recurrence, with a matching reverse recurrence for the backward pass; Legendre, Laguerre, Taylor and Pade expansions are studied as controls that isolate the roles of the basis and of the target function. On three fine-grained benchmarks and ImageNet-1k the decomposition-free logarithm is both faster and more accurate than the spectral logarithm and than the square-root approximations it replaces, and at matched basis and degree the log target beats the square-root target, confirming that the gain comes from the faithful Riemannian map rather than from a better polynomial family.

</details>


### [74] [Counterfactual Contrastive Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19032)
*Yunlong He,Pietro Gori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于对比分析(CA)的、与分类器无关的视觉反事实解释(VCE)生成方法，通过解耦两个类别数据集中共享与各自特有的生成因子，仅交换特有因子来生成反事实图像。方法利用StyleGAN2的特征空间F（而非W空间）更好地保留细节，并扩展了传统CA框架以支持每个数据集多个特有因子。在三个医学影像数据集上验证了优越的反事实生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VCE方法本质上依赖分类器，因此容易受到分类器偏差和失效模式的影响，如对捷径特征的敏感性和校准误差。作者希望提出一种不依赖分类器、直接从数据分布出发的VCE方法，以避免上述问题。

Method: 基于对比分析(CA)框架，给定两个不同类别的数据集（如健康与患者），解耦两个数据集之间的公共生成因子与各自特有的生成因子，仅通过交换特有因子生成反事实图像。具体地：1) 使用StyleGAN2的合成质量和结构化潜空间；2) 使用F空间而非通常的W空间以改善细节保留；3) 针对VCE任务重新设计框架和损失函数，允许多个特有因子存在于每个数据集中（区别于传统CA通常假设仅有单个数据集具有特有因子）。

Result: 在三个医学影像数据集上的实验表明，该方法相比现有VCE方法实现了更优的反事实生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过在数据分布层面而非决策边界层面操作，该方法提供了模型无关的VCE，对分类器偏差敏感度更低，为反事实解释提供了一个更鲁棒、更通用的新范式。

Abstract: Visual Counterfactual Explanations (VCEs) aim to explain image classifiers by generating minimally edited and realistic versions of an input image that change the classifier's prediction. Existing VCE methods are inherently classifier-dependent and therefore susceptible to classifier biases and failure modes, such as sensitivity to shortcut features and calibration errors. In this paper, we propose a classifier-free approach for visual counterfactual generation based on Contrastive Analysis (CA). Given two datasets corresponding to different classes (e.g., healthy and patients), we disentangle the generative factors that are common across the two datasets from those that are salient to each dataset, and generate counterfactual images by swapping only the salient factors. By operating directly on data distributions rather than decision boundaries, our method provides model-agnostic VCEs that are less sensitive to classifier biases. Our approach leverages the high-quality synthesis and well-structured latent space of StyleGAN2. We use the feature space F, instead than the usual W-space, to improve detail preservation. Unlike conventional CA approaches, which typically assume salient factors in only one dataset, we introduce an adapted framework and loss functions for VCE that allow multiple salient factors in each dataset. We evaluate our method on three medical imaging datasets and demonstrate superior counterfactual generation quality compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [75] [USR-Drive: Unified Driving Scene Representation via Joint Denoising of 3D Gaussians and Boxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19036)
*Li-Heng Chen,Haokai Pang,Chengye Su,Jiarun Liu,Qifeng Chen,Ziqian Ni,Jianxin Huang,Shi-Sheng Huang,Hongbo Fu,Sheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: USR-Drive提出一个统一的条件生成框架，通过多模态扩散Transformer将密集3D高斯原语和稀疏3D边界框在共享场景表示中联合去噪，实现动态重建和实例级目标检测的相互增强，在nuScenes和VKitti数据集上两个任务均达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶方法将动态重建和实例级感知视为分离任务，尽管它们共享估计底层3D世界状态的共同目标。这导致动态重建缺乏约束（under-constrained），而3D检测缺乏几何依据（lacking geometric grounding）。先前范式中边界框要么作为外部条件、要么由独立模块预测，未能充分利用两类表示之间的互补性。

Method: USR-Drive将密集3D高斯原语和稀疏3D边界框表示为两个对齐的潜在token流，通过统一的多模态扩散Transformer联合去噪。提出Unified Positional Encoding (UPE)在共享的度量时空坐标中对齐异构token，使两类模态作为相互约束的状态变量而非外部条件，从而在统一生成框架中实现几何信息与实例先验的双向强化。

Result: 在nuScenes和VKitti两个数据集上，动态重建和3D检测两个任务均取得了最先进（SOTA）的结果。

Conclusion: 通过统一表示和生成式框架，密集几何为边界框预测提供度量证据，而边界框为时序3D几何表示提供实例级结构先验以保持空间一致性并减少歧义，验证了两类互补表示在自动驾驶场景中协同增强的有效性。

Abstract: Spatial representation learning for autonomous driving aims to map raw visual signals into structured 3D scene representations, where object-centric bounding boxes and rendering-oriented 3D primitives (\eg, 3D Gaussians) serve as two distinct yet highly complementary levels for scene understanding. Existing methods typically treat dynamic reconstruction and instance-level perception as separate tasks, despite their shared goal of estimating the underlying 3D world state. As a result, dynamic reconstruction is under-constrained while 3D detection lacks geometric grounding. To address this gap, we propose USR-Drive, a unified conditional generative framework that, given only posed multi-view driving videos, jointly recovers dense dynamic geometry and instance-level object layouts within a shared scene representation. Specifically, USR-Drive represents dense Gaussian primitives and sparse 3D bounding boxes as two aligned latent token streams and jointly denoises them with a unified multi-modal diffusion Transformer. Unlike prior paradigms that use boxes as external conditions or predict them with detached modules, USR-Drive treats them as mutually constrained state variables with a Unified Positional Encoding (UPE) that aligns heterogeneous tokens within a shared metric spatiotemporal coordinate. Via such unified representation and generative framework, the two modalities reinforce each other: geometry supplies dense metric evidence for box prediction, while boxes provide instance-level structural priors that help preserve spatial consistency and reduce ambiguity in sequential 3D geometric representation. Our approach successfully delivers state-of-the-art results for both dynamic reconstruction and 3D detection on the nuScenes and VKitti datasets.

</details>


### [76] [Generalized Audio-Driven Synthesis of Precise Drummer Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19055)
*Álvaro G. Iñesta,Mattia Ryffel,Amit H. Bermano,Robert W. Sumner,Martin Guay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个生成式扩散框架，通过双目标损失函数解耦骨骼完整性与鼓棒精度，实现音频驱动的厘米级精准击鼓动画生成，并提出两种新评估指标（击打目标距离和音频-动作相关性分数）以量化时空精度，在野生音频上具备良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐驱动的角色动画在娱乐和交互式教育中具有重要应用，但从音频合成逼真的击鼓动作仍具挑战。难点在于高加速度动力学与极端时空精度之间的内在张力，而现有方法（如动作匹配或依赖MIDI）难以泛化到多样的真实世界音频，且缺乏能够区分精准击鼓与噪声动作的标准化评估指标。

Method: 提出一个生成式扩散框架，包含以下关键组件：(1) 双目标损失函数，将骨骼完整性约束与鼓棒精度约束解耦，在保持自然身体动态的同时实现厘米级鼓棒精度；(2) 自建数据集与数据增强策略，使模型能够泛化至非整理的真实世界（in-the-wild）音频；(3) 两种新评估指标：impact-to-target距离用于量化空间精度，audio-motion correlation score用于评估时间对齐。

Result: 定量分析和用户研究均表明该系统生成的高质量动作在多数情况下与真实演奏动作难以区分，有效验证了模型在空间精度和时间对齐上的优越性能。

Conclusion: 该工作通过扩散模型与双目标损失设计，成功解决了音频驱动击鼓动画中"自然度"与"精准度"的平衡难题，并首次建立了针对击鼓动作时空精度的标准化评估体系，为音乐驱动角色动画领域提供了新的方法与基准。

Abstract: Music-driven character animation enables and enhances transformative applications in entertainment and interactive education. However, synthesizing realistic drumming motion from audio remains challenging due to the inherent tension between high-acceleration dynamics and the need for extreme spatial-temporal precision. Existing approaches, often reliant on motion matching or MIDI input, struggle with generalizing to diverse real-world audio. Moreover, the field lacks standardized evaluation metrics capable of distinguishing precise drumming from noisy motion. In this paper, we introduce a generative diffusion framework featuring a dual-objective loss function that decouples skeletal integrity from drumstick precision, thus enabling centimeter-level stick precision without sacrificing natural body dynamics. Additionally, leveraging our own dataset and data augmentation strategy, the model generalizes to non-curated, in-the-wild audio. To rigorously evaluate performance, we propose two novel metrics: an impact-to-target distance to quantify spatial precision and an audio-motion correlation score to assess temporal alignment. Our quantitative analysis and user studies demonstrate that our system generates high-quality motion that is often indistinguishable from ground-truth performances.

</details>


### [77] [When Two Tracers Disagree: An Investigation of Multimodal Fusion for Clinical PET/CT Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19063)
*Jack A. Johnson,Bartłomiej W. Papież*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了PSMA和FDG PET/CT双示踪剂图像的多种多模态融合深度学习架构（早融合OEOD/OETD与中间融合DECA-UNet），用于全身病变分割和肿瘤总负荷估计。结果表明，单示踪剂3D nnU-Net基线性能最强（PSMA Dice=0.93，FDG=0.81），而融合策略虽在PSMA上表现合理但FDG性能下降，未能一致超越单示踪剂基线，提示需要更好保留示踪剂特异表征的架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PSMA与FDG PET/CT在前列腺癌中提供互补的生物学信息，双示踪剂融合可能捕获单独使用任一示踪剂所遗漏的异质性肿瘤表型，但目前缺乏有效的深度学习融合架构共识，需要系统评估不同融合策略的性能。

Method: 使用公开的DEEP-PSMA Challenge数据集，训练示踪剂特异的3D nnU-Net作为基线，并比较三种融合策略：(i) 早融合-单编码器单解码器（OEOD）；(ii) 早融合-单编码器双解码器（OETD）；(iii) 中间融合-双编码器交叉注意力U-Net（DECA-UNet），用于全身PET/CT病变分割。

Result: 单示踪剂基线表现优异（PSMA Dice=0.93，FDG=0.81）。融合结果参差不齐：OEOD在非示踪剂特异的较简单任务上综合Dice为0.90；示踪剂特异融合模型OETD达到PSMA/FDG=0.69/0.64，DECA-UNet为0.76/0.57。融合虽在PSMA分割上表现合理，但FDG性能下降，没有任何策略能一致超越单示踪剂基线。

Conclusion: 在评估的设置下，示踪剂特异模型仍是更优基线；有临床价值的融合增益需要能够更好保留示踪剂特异表征的架构。代码已开源。

Abstract: PSMA and FDG PET/CT visualise complementary biological information in prostate cancer. Combining both tracers could capture heterogeneous tumour phenotypes that may be missed by either alone, yet there is no consensus on effective deep learning architectures for fusing these modalities. We evaluated multimodal image-fusion strategies for automatic whole-body PET/CT lesion segmentation to estimate total tumour burden. Using the public DEEP-PSMA Challenge dataset, we trained tracer-specific 3D nnU-Net baselines and compared (i) early fusion with a single encoder and one decoder (OEOD) or two decoders (OETD), and (ii) intermediate fusion via a dual-encoder cross-attention U-Net (DECA-UNet). Tracer-specific baselines performed strongly (PSMA Dice = 0.93; FDG = 0.81). Fusion yielded mixed results: OEOD produced a combined Dice of 0.90 (on an easier, non-tracer-specific task), whilst the tracer-specific fusion models reached PSMA/FDG = 0.69/0.64 (OETD) and 0.76/0.57 (DECA-UNet). Whilst fusion often provided reasonable PSMA segmentation, FDG performance degraded and no strategy consistently exceeded the single-tracer baselines. Under the evaluated setting, tracer-specific models remain the stronger baseline; clinically useful gains from multimodal fusion will likely require architectures that better preserve tracer specific representations. Our code is available at: https://github.com/JackJ3636/DEEP_PSMA_code

</details>


### [78] [GS-VLA: Plug-and-Play Viewpoint Canonicalization for Frozen VLA Policies via Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19066)
*Yechan Park,HyunJin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GS-VLA，一个轻量级即插即用框架，通过3D高斯基元的新视角合成技术，在不重新训练VLA策略的情况下提升其对视角偏移的鲁棒性。实验表明，即使摄像头的微小位移也可能将LIBERO基准上的成功率从约90%降至约10%，而GS-VLA以仅4M参数的视觉模块恢复大部分性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略隐式假设训练与部署时的相机配置相同，但现实中视角偏移（如相机支架的微小位移）会严重降低策略性能。先前的方法如大规模微调或生成式数据增强计算代价高昂且存在灾难性遗忘风险，因此需要一种无需策略重训练即可应对视角偏移的轻量方案。

Method: 将视角偏移重新表述为局部化的新视角合成问题。基于"局部性假设"（相机扰动限于工作空间附近的有界区域），将视角归一化简化为与场景和策略无关的去遮挡任务。具体实现为一个仅4M参数的3D高斯基元canonicalizer模块，前置（prepend）于冻结的VLA策略之前，实现即插即用的观测空间自适应。

Result: GS-VLA在不修改策略权重的前提下，沿三个正交维度提升性能：（1）不同的策略架构；（2）未见过的任务套件；（3）不同尺度的扰动。实验显示该方法能恢复在视角偏移下丢失的大部分性能。

Conclusion: 一个轻量级视觉模块即可在无需策略重训练的条件下恢复视角偏移导致的大部分性能损失，验证了基于3D高斯基元新视角合成用于VLA观测空间自适应的有效性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a lightweight, plug-and-play framework that improves robustness to viewpoint shifts in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies without policy retraining. To our knowledge, this is the first approach to directly leverage 3D Gaussian-based novel-view synthesis for observation-space adaptation in VLA policies. Current VLA performance relies on the implicit assumption that training and deployment camera configurations are identical. Our experiments show that even a small displacement of the camera mount can reduce the success rate on the LIBERO benchmark from about 90% to about 10% in the worst case. Prior approaches, such as large-scale fine-tuning or generative data augmentation, are computationally expensive and risk catastrophic forgetting. To address this, viewpoint shifts are reformulated as a localized novel-view synthesis problem. Under a Locality assumption, that camera perturbations remain within a small bounded region relative to the workspace, viewpoint normalization reduces to a scene- and policy-independent disocclusion task. Our work implements this idea with a 4M-parameter 3D-Gaussian canonicalizer prepended to a frozen VLA policy. Without modifying policy weights, GS-VLA improves performance across three orthogonal axes: (1) Policy architectures, (2) Unseen task suites, and (3) Perturbation scales. These results show that a lightweight visual module can recover a large fraction of the performance lost under viewpoint shift, without policy retraining.

</details>


### [79] [ReWEIGH the Evidence: Calibrating Token-Level Ordinal Visual Evidence to Mitigate Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19075)
*Jihae Jeong,Junha Choi,Hwanjo Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReWEIGH，一种无需训练的推理时解码干预方法，通过聚合视觉位置的词汇排名证据并与从无标注图像估计的token特定参考值比较，在解码时惩罚低于参考值的候选token，从而减少LVLM的幻觉。在4个7B骨干模型上将幻觉对象提及减少高达21.3%，额外延迟仅1.33%/token，且适用于6种架构至32B参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）经常生成输入图像不支持的幻觉内容。防止这类内容需要每个候选token的图像支持强度度量。视觉token状态通过输出头投影可提供此证据，但位置级读数无法直接池化（概率幅度跨位置不可比），而词汇排名虽提供规模不变的池化基础，但token在典型排名证据上存在系统性差异，需要token特定的校正。

Method: ReWEIGH是一种无需训练的解码干预方法：1）聚合各视觉位置的词汇排名作为证据；2）从无标注图像估计每个token特定的参考证据值以消除token间的系统性差异；3）在推理时，prefill阶段缓存图像证据，对低于参考值的候选token施加有界惩罚。

Result: 在四个7B骨干模型上，ReWEIGH将幻觉对象提及减少高达21.3%，同时基本保持或提升描述性和一般性能。缓存证据后，平均每个token仅增加1.33%延迟；方法在六个架构族上均有效，最高适用至32B参数。

Conclusion: ReWEIGH通过聚合视觉位置排名证据并与token特定参考比较，在不进行额外训练的情况下有效减少LVLM幻觉，具有低延迟开销和跨架构、跨规模的泛化能力。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) often hallucinate, generating content that the input image does not support. Preventing such content during decoding calls for a candidate-specific measure of how strongly the image supports the token under consideration. The model's visual-token states offer a natural source of this evidence because projecting each state through the output head reveals which vocabulary items that position favors. These position-wise readouts cannot be pooled directly because their probability magnitudes are not comparable across visual positions. Vocabulary ranks provide a scale-invariant basis for pooling, but tokens still differ systematically in their typical rank-based evidence. We propose ReWEIGH, a training-free decoding intervention that aggregates these ranks across visual positions and compares each candidate with a token-specific reference estimated from unlabeled images. At inference, ReWEIGH caches the image evidence during prefill and applies a bounded penalty only to candidates that fall below their reference. On four 7B backbones, ReWEIGH reduces hallucinated object mentions by up to 21.3% while largely preserving or improving descriptive and general performance. With evidence cached, the average added latency is 1.33% per token, and the reductions extend across six architecture families to 32B parameters.

</details>


### [80] [Subgroup performance analysis of adaptation strategies for chest X-ray foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19078)
*Dhruv Gupta,Emma A. M. Stanley,Fabio De Sousa Ribeiro,Sujal Desai,Ben Glocker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了三种参数高效微调策略（线性头、MLP、注意力池化）应用于冻结的Rad-DINO胸部X光编码器时，对病理分类性能和子组公平性的影响。研究发现注意力池化性能最优但编码受保护属性（尤其是种族）最强，然而更强的属性编码并不对应更大的性能差距，且整体性能提升不能保证公平性改善，公平性影响需逐任务直接评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型越来越多地应用于医学影像下游任务，但不同的适配策略对子组公平性的影响尚未被充分研究。理解适配策略如何影响不同人口统计学子组（如种族、性别、影像视角）的模型表现差异，对于确保公平的医学影像AI系统至关重要。

Method: 在冻结的Rad-DINO胸部X光编码器上应用三种参数高效适配技术：原始CLS令牌上的线性头、MLP、以及基于多层patch特征的注意力池化模块。使用MIMIC-CXR数据集，在保持患病率且人口统计学平衡的测试集上评估八种病理，涵盖种族、性别和影像视角子组，并探测各适配器对受保护属性的编码强度。

Result: 注意力池化在整体判别性能上最强，且对受保护属性（尤其是种族）的编码最强。但改进的整体性能并不能一致地减少子组差异。值得注意的是，更强的属性编码并不对应更大的差异：早期网络层编码种族最弱却产生了最大的子组性能差距。探索不同注意力池化层组合时，未发现池化层选择、属性编码强度和子组公平性之间存在一致关系。

Conclusion: 更丰富、更具表达力的表征可以提高准确率，但公平性影响是任务依赖且不可预测的。必须直接且逐任务地评估公平性，而不能仅从属性编码强度或整体性能推断。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly adapted for downstream medical imaging tasks, yet the influence of the chosen adaptation strategy on subgroup fairness remains poorly understood. We investigate how three parameter-efficient adaptation techniques, including linear heads on the raw CLS token, an MLP, and an attention-pooling module over multi-layer patch features, affect both pathology classification performance and subgroup disparities when applied to the frozen Rad-DINO chest X-ray encoder. Using MIMIC-CXR, we evaluate eight pathologies across race, sex, and imaging-view subgroups on a prevalence-preserving, demographically balanced test set, and additionally probe how strongly each adapter encodes protected attributes. We find that attention pooling achieves the strongest overall discriminative performance and encodes attributes, particularly race, most strongly, but that improved overall performance does not consistently reduce subgroup disparities. Notably, stronger attribute encoding did not correspond to larger disparities: early network layers encoded race most weakly yet produced the largest subgroup performance gaps. Exploring different attention-pooling layer combinations further revealed no consistent relationship between the layers pooled, attribute encoding strength, and subgroup fairness. Our results indicate that richer, more expressive representations can improve accuracy while leaving fairness implications task-dependent and unpredictable, which must be assessed directly and per-task rather than inferred from encoding strength or overall performance alone.

</details>


### [81] [SPK: Eliciting Structured Prior Knowledge for Interpretable Out-of-Distribution Detection in Real-Time Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19080)
*Changshun Wu,Weicheng He,Xiaowei Huang,Saddek Bensalem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPK框架，通过显式挖掘预训练目标检测器中隐式编码的潜在先验知识（包括部件级语义、几何和上下文先验），构建紧凑的五维SPK表示用于OOD检测，在多种检测器架构和基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标检测器对训练类别之外的物体常产生过度自信的预测（OOD幻觉）。现有方法要么直接在检测器表示上构建评分函数，要么修改检测器本身来抑制幻觉，但检测器表示中隐式编码的潜在先验知识仍未被显式解码用于OOD检测。作者希望挖掘这些被忽视的潜在先验。

Method: SPK框架利用分布内数据和幻觉诱导样本作为诊断监督，显式激发目标检测器决策过程中的部件级语义概念（而不是仅用于拒绝或检测器适配）。随后将语义先验与几何和上下文先验整合，形成紧凑的五维SPK表示用于OOD检测。

Result: 在多种目标检测器架构和多个OOD基准上的大量实验表明，SPK取得了SOTA的OOD检测性能。实验发现预训练目标检测器编码的潜在知识远比通常用于OOD检测的更为丰富，且这些知识可以被显式激发并组织成紧凑、结构化、可解释的知识空间。

Conclusion: 预训练目标检测器中隐式编码的潜在先验可以被显式地挖掘并组织为紧凑可解释的知识空间，用于预测可靠性分析。这为提升目标检测器可靠性提供了一条主动式的有效路径，而不仅仅是被动地检测或抑制幻觉。

Abstract: Object detectors often produce over-confident predictions for objects outside their training categories, leading to so-called out-of-distribution (OoD) hallucinations. Existing approaches for detecting or mitigating such hallucinations typically either construct scoring functions directly over learned object detector representations or modify the object detector itself to suppress hallucination emergence. However, the latent priors implicitly encoded in these representations remain largely unexplored and have not been explicitly decoded for OoD detection. To uncover and exploit these latent priors, we propose Structured Prior Knowledge (SPK), a hallucination-oriented framework that explicitly elicits OoD-relevant priors from pretrained object detectors. Specifically, SPK leverages in-distribution data and hallucination-inducing samples as diagnostic supervision to elicit part-level semantic concepts underlying object detector decision-making, rather than using them merely for rejection or object detector adaptation. The elicited semantic priors are further integrated with geometric and contextual priors to form a compact five-dimensional SPK representation for OoD detection. Extensive experiments across diverse object detector architectures and multiple OoD benchmarks demonstrate that SPK achieves state-of-the-art OoD detection. Our findings reveal that pretrained object detectors already encode substantially richer latent knowledge than is typically exploited for OoD detection. More importantly, this knowledge can be explicitly elicited and organized into a compact, structured, and interpretable knowledge space for prediction reliability analysis. This suggests a promising proactive route for improving object detector reliability by explicitly uncovering and leveraging latent priors. Code and data are available at: https://gricad-gitlab.univ-grenoble-alpes.fr/dnn-safety/spk

</details>


### [82] [Detecting Backdoors in Object Detection via Pre-NMS Prediction Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19088)
*Longtian Wang,Zhengyu Zhao,Chenhao Lin,Le Yang,Shiwei Wang,Yuhan Zhi,Xiaofei Xie,Chao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DistScan是一种基于预NMS类别预测分布与训练类别频率偏差来检测后门攻击的框架，无需模型权重访问、触发器知识或额外训练，在MS-COCO和PASCAL VOC上相比最佳基线平均检测准确率提升27.32个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门检测方法（如触发器反转或基于架构假设的方法）在场景级攻击下泛化能力差。场景级攻击中单个触发器可同时导致场景中所有对象产生异常行为，而现有方法无法可靠检测此类攻击，需要一种更通用、无需模型内部信息或触发器知识的检测方案。

Method: DistScan基于一个未被探索的观察：后门注入会系统性地使模型在pre-NMS阶段的类别预测分布偏离其在训练阶段的类别频率，即使在无触发器的干净输入上也是如此。该方法在干净验证集上聚合中间类别预测，若结果分布与训练类别频率显著偏离，则判定模型被后门攻击。该方法不需要模型权重访问、无需触发器知识、无需额外训练。

Result: 在MS-COCO和PASCAL VOC数据集上，跨两种架构和三种场景级攻击场景的广泛实验中，DistScan显著优于现有方法，平均检测准确率相比最佳适用基线提升27.32个百分点。

Conclusion: DistScan通过利用后门注入导致的类别分布偏移这一简单而有效的信号，提供了一种通用、轻量且无需模型内部访问的后门检测方案，有效解决了现有方法在场景级攻击下泛化不足的问题。

Abstract: Object detection models deployed in safety-critical applications remain vulnerable to backdoor attacks that cause targeted misbehaviors when a hidden trigger is present. Existing detection methods either rely on trigger inversion or exploit architecture-specific assumptions, and critically, representative existing methods fail to generalize reliably to scene-level attacks, where a single trigger induces anomalous behavior across all objects in the scene simultaneously. We present DistScan, a backdoor detection framework based on a simple but previously unexploited observation: backdoor injection systematically shifts a model's pre-NMS prediction class distribution away from its training class frequencies, even on clean inputs without any trigger present. DistScan aggregates intermediate class predictions over a clean validation set and flags a model as backdoored if the resulting distribution deviates significantly from the training class frequencies, requiring no model weight access, no trigger knowledge, and no additional training. Extensive experiments on MS-COCO and PASCAL VOC across two architectures and three scene-level attack scenarios demonstrate that DistScan substantially outperforms existing methods, improving average detection accuracy over the best-performing applicable baseline by 27.32 percentage points.

</details>


### [83] [Image-Guided Pavement Defect Recognition in GPR Data with novel 3D Deep Learning Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19177)
*Yuandong Pan,Linjun Lu,Mudan Wang,Florian Noichl,Fan Xue,Brian Sheil,Lavindra de Silva,André Borrmann,Ioannis Brilakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合正射影像RGB与3D探地雷达(GPR)数据的低成本标注流程，用于大规模生成带注释的3D GPR路面缺陷数据集，并设计了一个包含残差连接、混合卷积核及双重注意力机制的3D CNN模型，在修补和裂缝缺陷的二分类任务中优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPR在路面自动检测中面临两大挑战：一是真实世界中带标注的3D GPR数据集稀缺，二是缺乏针对3D GPR数据特性的深度学习模型。现有方法难以支撑大规模自动化检测应用。

Method: 首先提出一种低成本数据准备流程，利用路面表面RGB正射影像与3D GPR扫描的对齐段，将表面可见缺陷标签迁移至对应GPR段，实现对运营中高速公路真实数据的高效大规模标注。其次设计了一种专门的3D CNN架构，包含残差连接、混合卷积核尺寸、深度注意力与通道注意力机制，以增强特征表示和缺陷分类能力。

Result: 在路面修补和裂缝缺陷的二分类任务上，所提出的网络在多项评估指标上优于基线架构；消融研究证实了各设计模块的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种可扩展且实用的真实世界3D GPR数据集生成方法，以及一个新颖的深度学习框架，为大规模自动化路面检测提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Ground Penetrating Radar (GPR) is a widely adopted non-destructive sensing technology for subsurface inspection in civil and transportation engineering. Despite its potential for pavement condition assessment, the large-scale application of GPR in automated inspection has two key challenges: the scarcity of annotated real-world datasets and the lack of deep learning models designed for the unique characteristics of 3-Dimensional (3D) GPR data. This study addresses these limitations by firstly introducing a cost-effective data preparation pipeline that integrates orthomosaic Red Green Blue (RGB) imagery with 3D GPR scans to generate annotated 3D GPR datasets. The proposed method uses the aligned segments of RGB and GPR data, using pavement surface images as a reference to transfer labels of surface-visible defects to corresponding GPR segments, enabling efficient large-scale annotation in a real-world dataset collected on a highway section under operation. In addition to the dataset contribution, we propose a specialised 3D Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) architecture incorporating residual connections, mixed convolutional kernel sizes, and both depthwise and channelwise attention mechanisms to enhance feature representation and defect classification. The model is evaluated on binary classification tasks for detecting patch and crack defects in pavement structures. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed network outperforms baseline architectures across multiple evaluation metrics. Ablation studies further confirm the effectiveness of the designed architectural components. This work contributes a scalable and practical method for real-world dataset generation, along with a novel deep learning framework.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [84] [Position: Collusion Risks Among AI Reasoning Agents Justify Certification Requirements for Making Market Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18078)
*Matthew Riemer,Tommaso Tosato,Amin Memarian,Maximilian Puelma Touzel,Glen Berseth,Irina Rish,Guillaume Dumas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了具有链式推理能力的AI智能体先天倾向于产生合谋行为，并主张在将其部署到影响经济市场的决策场景前必须获得行为认证。实验表明DeepSeek-R1智能体在伯特兰寡头定价场景中倾向于默示合谋，且这种合谋行为即使在人类提示其不要合谋时仍然存在，且其推理轨迹可以通过语义上不可检测的方式被操纵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着推理型AI智能体被集成到经济市场中，它们可能打破法律上区分竞争与合谋的证据标准，尽管经济危害上仍然存在区别——即市场可能出现合谋结果却没有任何共谋证据或意图。这种"无证据的合谋"对现有竞争法框架构成根本性挑战，需要提前识别风险并提出解决方案。

Method: 作者在伯特兰寡头定价博弈中实验DeepSeek-R1智能体，观察其在面对竞争环境时的定价行为；通过人类提示干预测试合谋倾向的顽固性；操纵智能体的链式推理过程以测试推理轨迹是否可以被语义检测出来；并提出基于行为认证的治理框架作为解决方案。

Result: 实验发现：(1) DeepSeek-R1智能体表现出默示合谋倾向，且该倾向在人类明确提示其不要合谋时依然存在；(2) 链式推理可以被引导至极端合谋或极端竞争行为，且这种引导在语义上无法被另一个LLM检测；(3) 提供了初步证据表明可以通过泛化方式将智能体引导至有效竞争均衡；(4) 但全面的行为认证体系在部署前仍属必要。

Conclusion: 部署推理型AI智能体于市场决策将导致合谋的经济结果却无任何共谋或意图的证据，因此必须建立基于代表性情境中观察行为的行为认证机制。虽然存在将智能体引导至竞争性均衡的可能性，但在真实市场部署前仍需开发全面的行为认证框架以确保市场稳定与效率。

Abstract: This position paper argues that AI agents with chain-of-thought reasoning capabilities are predisposed to exhibit collusive behavior and should be required to obtain behavioral certification before making decisions that affect economic markets. This is because integrating these agents into society could collapse the legal evidentiary distinction between competition and collusion among independent firms without eroding the economic harm distinction. Experiments with DeepSeek-R1 agents in the Bertrand oligopoly pricing domain reveal a tendency towards tacit collusion that persists even when humans prompt the agents not to collude. We further show that the chain-of-thought of these agents can be steered toward either extremely collusive or highly competitive behavior in a way that is not semantically detectable by another LLM analyzing the reasoning traces. As a result, deploying reasoning agents for market decisions leads to collusive economic outcomes without any evidence of conspiracy or intent. Thus, certification based on observed behavior in representative situations is necessary to prevent collusion. We provide preliminary evidence that such agents can be steered in a generalizable way toward efficient competitive equilibria. However, developing a comprehensive behavioral certification will be required before these models can be deployed in real-world markets while ensuring their stability and efficiency.

</details>


### [85] [Position: Profiling Game Worlds by Transition Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18079)
*Lele Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出转移复杂度剖面（TCP），一组可复现的度量指标，用于量化游戏世界建模与强化学习环境中底层转移预测问题的难度，并呼吁将其作为标准基准元数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏世界建模（GWM）与强化学习（RL）研究常混淆，原因在于论文很少量化在声明接口（像素/令牌/潜变量及有限历史）下底层转移预测问题的难度。缺乏统一度量导致不同基准间的结果难以比较。

Method: TCP由三部分组成：（i）内在单步分支因子；（ii）交互引发的不确定性（可观测时的对手影响）；（iii）通过标准化探针曲线衡量的时间/空间依赖跨度。TCP报告时附带显式参考分布、协议随机性以及版本化测量预算（采样/重采样与固定探针计算量），从而跨基准可比。

Result: 论文概述了常见游戏族与现代"神经游戏引擎"领域如何分布在该度量谱系中，展示了TCP作为标准化的环境复杂度描述工具的适用性。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁将TCP确立为GWM和RL论文的标准基准元数据及必需统计量，以促进不同环境与方法之间的公平比较和可复现研究。

Abstract: Game world modeling (GWM) and reinforcement learning (RL) are often confounded because research papers rarely quantify how difficult the underlying transition prediction problem is at the declared interface (pixels/tokens/latents with finite history). We propose the Transition Complexity Profile (TCP): a small, reproducible set of metrics that characterizes an environment's (or gameplay dataset's) induced transition kernel by (i) intrinsic one-step branching, (ii) interaction-induced uncertainty and opponent influence when observable, and (iii) temporal/spatial dependency span via standardized probe curves. TCP is reported with an explicit reference distribution, protocol stochasticity, and a versioned measurement budget (sampling/resampling and fixed probe compute), enabling comparable numbers across benchmarks. We outline how common game families and modern "neural game engine" domains populate this landscape and call for TCP to become standard benchmark metadata and a required statistic in GWM and RL papers.

</details>


### [86] [Large Language Models in Mental Health: A Systematic Review of Applications, Innovations, and Ethical Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18080)
*Yisong Chen,Yifan Gao,Sijing Yu,Chuqing Zhao,Yang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于大语言模型在心理健康领域应用的综述，涵盖社交媒体分析、临床对话代理、治疗支持工具、提示工程、多模态学习及伦理考量，并强调其在抑郁症早期检测、自杀风险评估、个性化治疗支持和心理教育内容生成方面的进展与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理健康问题的早期检测和干预是重大公共卫生需求，而大语言模型在自然语言理解和多模态数据处理方面展现出巨大潜力。作者通过整合跨学科研究发现，系统梳理LLM在心理健康领域的应用现状，旨在为临床实践和政策制定提供依据。

Method: 采用综述性研究方法，整合来自社交媒体帖子、电子病历和多模态输入等多样化数据源的跨学科研究，系统梳理LLM在心理健康领域的应用分类，包括模型架构、注释策略、提示工程、多模态融合技术以及伦理监管框架。

Result: 综述突出了LLM模型和注释策略在可解释性和临床相关性方面的进步，展示了多模态融合技术（文本、语音、传感器数据）对心理健康诊断和监测的改善作用，同时指出了提示工程在领域适应中的关键作用以及持续的伦理、社会技术和监管挑战。

Conclusion: 作者倡导建立确保LLM在真实心理卫生保健中安全、公平和负责任部署的框架，强调需要持续关注伦理、社会技术和监管层面问题，以推动LLM在心理健康领域的可靠应用。

Abstract: We present a review on the applications of large language models (LLMs) in health, e.g., social media analysis, clinical conversational agents, therapy support tools, prompt engineering, multimodal learning, and ethical considerations. We integrate findings from interdisciplinary studies utilizing diverse data sources such as social media posts, electronic medical records, and multimodal inputs to enable early detection of depression, suicide risk assessment, personalized therapy support, and psychoeducational content generation. Our review highlights advancements in LLM models and annotation strategies that enhance interpretability and clinical relevance, while we also emphasize the critical role of prompt engineering for domain adaptation. We also discuss emerging multimodal fusion techniques integrating text, speech, and sensor data for improved mental health diagnosis and monitoring. Finally, we address ongoing ethical, sociotechnical, and regulatory challenges, and advocate frameworks to ensure safe, equitable, and accountable deployment of LLMs in real-world mental health care.

</details>


### [87] [Position: Behavioral Systems Require Behavioral Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18081)
*Manuel Cherep,Nikhil Singh,Pattie Maes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张AI代理系统应像其他行为系统一样被评估，即通过系统性的观察、扰动和行动解释来理解其行为过程，而非仅关注性能结果，并提出建立"AI行为科学"的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI代理系统的评估方法主要聚焦于性能结果（perfomance outcomes），忽略了产生这些结果的底层行为过程。随着AI代理越来越多地作为行为系统运行——与动态环境交互、追求目标、随时间适应——需要更全面、更科学的评估范式来理解其行为。

Method: 论文借鉴行为科学的经验教训，提出三个核心研究方向：1) 从行动序列中恢复决策策略的方法；2) 构建能够隔离行为差异的环境；3) 探测多代理系统中的涌现动力学。通过这些方向形成严格的"行为测试"研究议程。

Result: 该论文提出了一项研究路线图，旨在建立"AI行为科学"（a science of AI behavior），以系统性的行为观察、扰动和解释方法，替代当前仅以结果为导向的评估体系。

Conclusion: AI代理的评估应当从"结果导向"转向"过程导向"，借鉴行为科学的成熟方法论，构建系统性的行为测试框架，从而更深入地理解和解释AI代理的行为机制与涌现特性。

Abstract: Artificial agentic systems increasingly operate as behavioral systems by interacting with dynamic environments, pursuing goals, and adapting over time. Yet, current evaluation methods largely focus on performance outcomes, not the underlying behavioral processes that produce them. This paper argues that AI agents must be evaluated like other behavioral systems: through systematic observation, perturbation, and interpretation of their actions. We draw on lessons from the behavioral sciences to motivate this position, and propose a research agenda focused on developing rigorous behavioral tests. These include methods for recovering decision strategies from action sequences, constructing environments that isolate behavioral differences, and probing emergent dynamics in multi-agent systems. Taken together, these directions offer a roadmap for developing a science of AI behavior.

</details>


### [88] [Position: Current Model Cards Are Insufficient for Downstream Governance of Open-Weight Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18086)
*Sungwon Chae,Keonwoo Kim,Hoki Kim,Jaeyeon Ju,Sangchul Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于对Hugging Face上500个模型卡片（model cards）的分析，主张开放权重基础模型（OWFMs）的有效治理需要多层次方法，整合模型卡片、可接受使用政策（AUPs）和许可证三大互补组件，以填补现有监管框架中的安全空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重基础模型的快速增长促使AI社区重新评估下游治理策略。现有模型卡片框架虽然被广泛采用，但未能充分告知下游开发者和用户有关开放权重基础模型所特有的安全挑战，存在监管空白。

Method: 本文采用立场论文（position paper）的方法，通过分析Hugging Face平台上托管的500个模型卡片，识别出现有监管方法在模型谱系（model heritage）、对齐来源（alignment provenance）和经验观察行为（empirically observed behaviors）等方面的安全空白。

Result: 研究发现现有模型卡片在提供安全关键信息方面存在不足，且标准开源许可证（OSLs）不适合开放权重基础模型，可能削弱可接受使用政策的可执行性（enforceability）。

Conclusion: 需要将模型卡片、可接受使用政策和许可证演化为整合的安全工件（integrated safety artifacts），构建一个将信息维度、规范维度和法律维度连贯整合的全面治理框架。

Abstract: The growth of open-weight foundation models (OWFMs) has prompted the AI community to re-evaluate strategies for effective downstream governance. Although model cards have been widely adopted as transparency artifacts in model repositories, existing frameworks often fail to adequately inform downstream developers and users about the distinct safety challenges posed by OWFMs. This position paper analyzes 500 model cards hosted on Hugging Face and argues that effective governance of OWFMs requires a multi-layered approach integrating three complementary components: (i) model cards, (ii) acceptable use policies (AUPs), and (iii) licenses. To motivate this claim, we identify a safety gap left by existing regulatory approaches, including model heritage, alignment provenance, and empirically observed behaviors, through an analysis of model cards with safety-critical information. We further argue that standard open-source licenses (OSLs) are not well suited for OWFMs and may weaken the enforceability of AUPs. Building on these observations, we outline directions for evolving model cards, AUPs, and licenses into integrated safety artifacts to enable a more comprehensive governance framework that coherently integrates informational, normative, and legal dimensions.

</details>


### [89] [A Metamorphic Artificial Age Score Decision-Support Prototype for Flight-Log-Based Drone Propeller Health Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18088)
*Seyma Yaman Kayadibi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于变质人工年龄评分（AAS）的决策支持原型系统，用于基于飞行日志的无人机螺旋桨健康监测。系统从MATLAB原始矩阵中计算六个健康指标（轨迹跟踪误差、姿态不稳定度、推力指令负担、电机指令不平衡、ESC指令不稳定性和电池电量压力），并以健康基线为参照进行归一化评分。通过2024年DronePropA公共数据集的回顾性评估，系统成功将健康案例分配至例行监控，将三种不同严重程度的螺旋桨故障案例分别分配至维护复核和强制检查。研究强调螺旋桨故障效应分布在多个操作通道，支持多指标决策支持层的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机螺旋桨故障的影响往往分布在多个飞行日志通道中，而非表现为单一诊断信号，这给安全性和可靠性带来风险。现有方法难以从跨通道的分布式故障特征中做出有效的维护优先决策，因此需要一种多指标融合的决策支持方案。

Method: 该方法从DronePropA公共数据集的真实飞行日志中提取六个健康相关指标（轨迹跟踪误差、姿态不稳定度、推力指令负担、电机指令不平衡、ESC指令不稳定性和电池电量压力），基于健康基线进行归一化处理，通过候选评分策略、变质充分性关系和冗余调整后的AAS公式进行评估。使用一个健康基线和三个缺陷螺旋桨案例在相同速度剖面和轨迹下进行受控回顾性评估。

Result: 健康案例被分配至例行监控；严重度1案例以ESC指令不稳定性为主导，被分配至维护复核；严重度2案例达到最大电机指令和ESC指令负担，严重度3案例达到最大轨迹跟踪误差，两者均触发强制检查。结果表明螺旋桨故障效应可通过不同操作通道显现。

Conclusion: 螺旋桨故障的影响具有跨通道分布特性，单一诊断信号不足以全面表征故障状态。所提出的多指标AAS决策支持层能够有效地对飞行后维护进行优先级排序，并为自主系统监督提供支撑，验证了多指标决策支持方法的必要性。

Abstract: Drone propeller faults can create safety and reliability risks when their effects are distributed across multiple flight-log channels rather than appearing as a single diagnostic signal. This paper proposes a Metamorphic Artificial Age Score (AAS) decision-support prototype for flight-log-based drone propeller health monitoring. Using selected historical real flight logs from the 2024 DronePropA public dataset, the framework computes six health-related indicators from raw MATLAB matrices: trajectory tracking error, attitude instability, thrust-command burden, motor-command imbalance, ESC-command instability, and battery-level stress. These indicators are normalized relative to a healthy baseline and evaluated through candidate scoring policies, metamorphic adequacy relations, and a redundancy-adjusted AAS formulation. In this context, AAS is used as a structural policy-adequacy and burden measure rather than as a chronological age measure. A controlled retrospective evaluation was performed using one healthy baseline and three defective propeller cases under the same speed profile and trajectory. The healthy case was assigned to routine monitoring. The Severity 1 case was dominated by ESC-command instability and assigned to maintenance review. The Severity 2 case reached maximum motor-command and ESC-command burden, while the Severity 3 case reached maximum trajectory tracking error; both triggered mandatory inspection. The results show that propeller fault effects may appear through different operational channels, supporting the need for a multi-indicator decision-support layer for post-flight maintenance prioritization and autonomous-system oversight.

</details>


### [90] [Position: Multi-Agent Systems Should Prioritize Concurrency Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18092)
*Xin Yang,Letian Li,Zimo Ji,Terry Jingchen Zhang,Wenyuan Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出观点性论述：LLM多智能体系统（MAS）中的许多失败本质上是并发控制问题，而非单纯的协调或通信问题，主张将并发控制作为MAS框架的一等设计考量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-based多智能体系统虽然承诺可扩展的协作，但增加智能体数量反而往往降低系统可靠性。现有研究多将失败归因于协调或通信问题，本文揭示这些失败可映射为经典的并发异常（脏读、丢失更新、不一致结果），从而指出根因在于并发控制缺失。

Method: 这是一篇position paper（观点性论文），通过将MAS中的失败模式与经典并发控制异常（stale reads、lost updates、inconsistent outcomes）进行映射分析，论证MAS失败的本质是并发控制问题，并提出需要显式的并发控制机制：冲突检测、隔离性保证、对共享资源的结构化访问。

Result: 论证了MAS框架应将并发控制作为一等设计关切，而非事后补救；提出通过冲突检测、隔离保证和结构化共享资源访问三类机制来应对MAS中的并发异常。

Conclusion: 并发控制应成为MAS框架设计的核心关注点，而非附加功能。MAS的可靠性提升需要将传统数据库/分布式系统的并发控制理论引入多智能体系统设计。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) promise scalable collaboration, yet adding agents often reduces reliability. This position paper argues that many MAS failures are fundamentally concurrency control problems: agents concurrently read and write shared state, and long LLM inference windows amplify the risk of stale reads, lost updates, and inconsistent outcomes. Failure modes commonly attributed to coordination or communication breakdowns can be mapped directly onto classical concurrency anomalies. We contend that MAS frameworks should address these failures through explicit concurrency control mechanisms: conflict detection, isolation guarantees, and structured access to shared resources. Concurrency control should be a first-class design concern, not an afterthought.

</details>


### [91] [FinSkillBench: Evaluating AI Agents and Domain Skills for Investment Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18099)
*Jermyn Zhen Yong Bek,Zhuang Qiang Bok,Zhongtian Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了FinSkillBench，一个专为投资管理领域设计的AI代理评估基准，包含3个领域、12个子任务和2,603个任务片段。比较了三种条件（无技能、精选技能包、自生成技能），横跨9个模型的大规模评估表明：精选技能包持续提升性能（平均分从0.366升至0.528），而自生成技能收益甚微且计算成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投资管理是高风险领域，AI代理系统不仅要生成文本，还需检索时点数据、组装计算输入、调用专业方法并产生可审计的结构化输出。现有评估缺乏对金融专业技能使用能力的系统化度量，因此需要专门的评估套件来衡量语言模型代理是否有效运用金融领域技能解决投资管理任务。

Method: 构建FinSkillBench评估套件，覆盖组合构建、风险管理和基本面分析三大领域，含12个子任务和2,603个任务片段。每个片段提供时点输入、隐藏真实标签和任务专属验证器。设计三种对比条件：无技能、精选技能包（程序文档+可执行组件）和自生成技能（代理自己编写并复用流程）。在9个模型上进行大规模评估，并使用独立的Hermes Agent框架（8个模型，5,280个片段）进行交叉验证。

Result: 精选技能包一致提升性能，平均分从0.366提升至0.528，在组合构建和风险管理领域提升最大。自生成技能收益甚微且计算成本更高。独立框架的复现实验在所有三个领域验证了相同的方向性模式，技能效果的幅度随子任务和测试框架变化。

Conclusion: 在投资管理代理中，可靠程序性技能的访问与模型选择同等重要，而朴素的自生成技能往往无效。研究发布了基准测试、评估工具、精选技能包和完整轨迹以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Investment management is a high-stakes domain in which agentic AI systems must do more than generate plausible text. They must retrieve point-in-time data, assemble correct computational inputs, invoke specialized methods, and produce auditable structured outputs. We introduce FinSkillBench, an evaluation suite designed to measure whether language model agents can effectively use financial domain skills to solve investment management tasks. The benchmark spans three domains, portfolio construction, risk management, and fundamental analysis, and includes 12 subtasks with 2,603 task episodes.
  Each episode provides point-in-time inputs, hidden ground truth, and a task-specific verifier.We compare three conditions: no skill, curated skill packages consisting of procedural documents and executable components, and self-generated skills in which the agent writes and reuses its own procedures within an episode. Across 9 models and a large-scale evaluation, curated skills consistently improve performance, raising mean scores from 0.366 to 0.528, with the largest gains in portfolio construction and risk management.
  In contrast, self-generated skills provide little benefit despite higher computational cost. An independent evaluation using a separate agent framework (Hermes Agent, 8 models, 5,280 episodes total) reproduces the directional pattern across all three domains, with the magnitude of skill effects varying by subtask and harness.
  These results showthat in investment management agents, access to reliable procedural skills can be as important as model choice, while naive self-generation of skills is often ineffective. We release the benchmark, evaluation tools, curated skill packages, and full trajectories to support further research.

</details>


### [92] [Self-Evolving Agents as Dynamic Graph Transformation: A Survey and New Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18104)
*Yuanyuan Xu,Wenjie Zhang,Yin Chen,Xuemin Lin,Ying Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将智能体（agent）的进化为动态图变换（dynamic graph transformation）的统一视角，通过将智能体状态建模为动态图（记忆、工具、技能、工作流、智能体间关系等作为类型化节点、边和子图），建立"自进化智能体"与"图学习"两个研究领域之间的桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体正逐渐演化为自进化系统，其状态具有结构性且动态变化。然而，现有研究存在裂谷：图智能体综述仅将图视为支撑结构而非进化基底；自进化智能体综述侧重智能体级机制而很少讨论图拓扑演化。这种将"不断演化的智能体状态"与"动态图拓扑"进行耦合的研究视角仍然缺失。

Method: 文章首先将智能体状态定义为动态图，其中记忆、工具、技能、工作流及智能体间关系表示为类型化节点、边和子图，并通过模式约束重写（schema-constrained rewrites）进行更新。基于此形式化定义，文章提出四类动态图方法的分类学：节点/特征演化、边/拓扑演化、子图激活、跨组件协同演化。进一步将九个动态图学习子领域映射到智能体演化能力，并讨论其适配与潜在失败模式。最后从动态图视角提出五类图感知的评估与治理协议。

Result: 文章产出包括四类自进化智能体的动态图方法分类体系、九个动态图学习子领域到智能体演化能力的映射（含适配与失败模式分析）、以及五类图感知的评估与治理协议，形成一套结构化的智能体设计与治理框架。

Conclusion: 该综述通过"智能体进化为动态图变换"的统一视角，为自进化智能体的设计与治理提供了一个紧凑的结构化透镜，弥合了图智能体与自进化智能体两个独立研究线之间的空白。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly becoming self-evolving systems that persist across interactions, maintain memories, use tools, acquire skills, refine workflows, and coordinate with other agents. These capabilities make agent states structural and dynamic: entities, relations, attributes, dependencies, and execution structures change with new evidence, feedback, and environmental conditions. Existing graph-agent surveys typically treat graphs as support structures for agent functions rather than as evolving substrates, while self-evolving-agent surveys focus on agent-level mechanisms and rarely discuss graph topology evolution. Thus, the coupling between evolving agent state and dynamic graph topology remains underexplored. This survey connects these two research lines by framing \textit{agent evolution as dynamic graph transformation}. We model agent state as a dynamic graph, where memories, tools, skills, workflows, and inter-agent relations are represented as typed nodes, edges, and subgraphs updated through schema-constrained rewrites. Based on this formulation, we organize existing dynamic-graph-based methods for self-evolving agents into four taxonomies: node/feature evolution, edge/topology evolution, subgraph activation, and cross-component co-evolution. Building on this taxonomy, we propose dynamic graph learning as reusable infrastructure for self-evolving agents and map nine dynamic-graph-learning subfields to agent-evolution capabilities, discussing their adaptations and possible failure modes. Finally, we discuss five types of graph-aware evaluation and governance protocols from a dynamic-graph perspective, which complement end-task evaluation. The goal is to provide a compact structural lens for designing and governing self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [93] [Emergence of Agentic AI: A Review on Evolution, Background, Working Principles, Applications, Adoption Factors, and Future Research Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18110)
*AKM Bahalul Haque,Al Amin Islam Ridoy,Mohammad Rayhan,Ivan Porres*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对智能体AI（Agentic AI）进行了系统性综述，涵盖其基本原理、历史演化、架构与工作机制、跨领域实际应用、研究现状分析、当前挑战及未来研究方向，并提出了一个包含系统质量维度的利益相关者采纳意愿综合框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI在人工智能领域正获得新的突破性进展，具有推动各领域快速变革的巨大潜力。这种快速进步和变革潜力促使人们需要深入理解并牢固掌握该技术，同时需要调查智能体AI的最新技术研究方向，以全面评估其改进和应用的潜在空间。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，考虑近期各领域发表的智能体AI学术贡献，通过以下步骤开展研究：讨论智能体AI的基础原理和工作机制，追溯人工系统中智能体的历史与理论演化，探索智能体AI的架构、工作原理和功能，考察其在各领域的实际应用，分析研究发现、识别当前挑战并讨论未来研究方向，最终提出基于系统质量维度的利益相关者采纳意图综合框架。

Result: 该综述为研究人员和实践者提供了对智能体AI的全面理解，涵盖其当前进展和应用，识别了关键研究空白，并勾勒出未来研究方向，同时提出了一个用于理解和预测利益相关者采纳智能体AI意图的综合框架。

Conclusion: 本文通过系统性综述为智能体AI领域提供了全面的知识整合，明确了当前技术发展状态、应用范围及待解决的研究问题，并为后续研究者指明了方向，同时所提出的采纳框架为智能体AI的实际推广与应用提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Agentic AI is gaining new insights and advancements in the field of Artificial Intelligence, fostering significant potential to enable rapid transformation across various domains.This rapid advancement and the potential to revolutionize various domains advocate the need for a deeper understanding and firm grasp of the technology. Moreover, an investigation into state of the art research directions in agentic AI needs to be conducted to comprehensively assess the potential scope for improvement and application.Therefore, to address these objectives, a comprehensive review can provide researchers and practitioners with valuable insights into the current state and future research scopes of agentic AI.Hence, this work considers the recently published scholarly contributions in agentic AI across various domains and discusses the fundamentals and working principles of Agentic AI, traces the historical and theoretical evolution of agency in artificial systems, explores and discusses Agentic AIs architecture, working principles, and functionalities, explores real-world applications of Agentic AI across various domains, analyzes the research findings, identifies current challenges, and discuss potential future research directions, and proposes a comprehensive framework of stakeholders intention to use and adopt Agentic AI with the help of proposed system quality dimensions.Therefore, this systematic review provides researchers and practitioners with a comprehensive understanding of Agentic AI, its current developments and applications, highlights key research gaps, and outlines future research directions.

</details>


### [94] [Solving Is Not Drawing: A Benchmark for Diagrammatic Reasoning in Olympiad Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18111)
*Hsien Xin Peng,Anthony Kim,Alvin Li,Calvin Supasanya,Shivank Garg,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个开源基准测试，包含954道奥林匹克几何问题（其中297道为困难子集），每道题配有人工撰写的高保真Asymptote代码图表，以及一套涵盖文本、代码、图像、VLM和基于约束的度量指标，专门用于评估模型的"图示推理"能力。研究发现当前基础模型在求解几何问题与绘制几何图形之间存在显著差距，平均编译成功率仅36.14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管GPT和Claude等基础模型在奥林匹克级数学问题上已表现出色，几何问题求解甚至成为评估模型数学推理能力的标准代理，但求解问题与绘制其所依赖的图形是两种不同技能。现有基准（如MathVista、MathVerse）仅衡量模型是否得出正确答案，没有专门隔离和度量模型构造几何图形本身的能力，因此这一关键能力未被测量。

Method: 构建了一个开源基准：954道自包含的奥林匹克几何问题（含297道困难子集），每道题配有其解答和人工编写的高保真、可渲染的Asymptote代码图表。同时设计了一套综合评估框架，包含基于文本、代码、图像、视觉语言模型（VLM）和约束的多种度量指标，用于评估'图示推理'能力。在多个当前基础模型上进行评测。

Result: 评测当前基础模型后发现，求解与绘图之间存在显著差距：模型生成的几何图形保真度明显不足，平均编译成功率仅为36.14%。这表明强大的数学推理能力并不意味着模型能够构建准确的几何图形。

Conclusion: 强数学推理并不蕴含准确几何作图能力，两者是独立的技能维度。该基准为社区提供了衡量模型图示推理能力的工具，填补了现有基准中这一能力的评估空白。数据和基准已在Hugging Face上开源。

Abstract: Foundation models such as GPT and Claude now solve olympiad-level mathematics with remarkable proficiency, so much so that geometry problem solving has become a standard proxy for their mathematical reasoning. Yet solving a geometry problem and drawing the figure it depends on are not the same skill: progress often hinges on a faithful diagram with the right auxiliary constructions and incidences, and it is unclear that a model which reasons its way to the answer can also produce one. A growing collection of benchmarks, including MathVista, and MathVerse, measures whether models reach the correct answer, but to our knowledge, none isolate the distinct ability to construct the diagram itself, leaving this capability unmeasured. We introduce an open-source benchmark that targets this gap: 954 self-contained olympiad geometry problems, with a 297-problem hard subset, each paired with its solution and a human-authored, high-fidelity diagram in renderable Asymptote code, together with a suite of text-, code-, image-, VLM-, and constraint-based metrics for what we term diagrammatic reasoning. Evaluating current foundation models reveals a pronounced gap between solving and drawing: their diagrams are markedly less faithful, with an average compile success rate of only 36.14\%. Strong mathematical reasoning, we find, does not imply the ability to construct accurate geometric diagrams. Our benchmark and dataset can be accessed at https://huggingface.co/datasets/max98765/hard_geometry_problems_with_diagrams.

</details>


### [95] [Position: AI Leaderboards Are Underserving the Global South: A Case Study from India](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18117)
*Sourav Banerjee,Saikat Saha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论述AI排行榜因缺乏独立治理、利益冲突政策和指标演化机制，结构性上无法服务全球南方地区。解决方案不是更多数据，而是从一开始就建立具有独立治理的区域性排行榜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球南方（以印度为例）虽然已有高质量的区域基准（如IndicSUPERB、MILU、LAHAJA、IrokoBench、AlGhafa），但这些基准未被全球排行榜纳入，且没有治理机制推动其纳入。商业压力只能纠正影响全球北方付费客户的排行榜缺陷，全球南方缺乏同等杠杆，导致影响印地语、斯瓦希里语或阿拉伯语用户的问题被记录后却长期得不到解决。

Method: 以印度为案例研究（14亿人口、22种法定语言、已有高质量基准但缺乏可信的聚合平台），通过对58位AI从业者的咨询调查，收集其对正式治理和基于披露的冲突管理机制的偏好。

Result: 咨询结果显示，58位AI从业者一致倾向于正式治理和基于披露的冲突管理机制。研究表明障碍不在于数据缺失而在于制度设计——现有高质量区域基准未被全球排行榜纳入，也缺乏治理机制强制纳入。

Conclusion: 解决全球南方AI评估问题的关键不是收集更多数据，而是建设更好的制度：从起点就建立具有独立治理的区域性排行榜，以弥补现有全球排行榜在机构设计上的结构性缺陷。

Abstract: This position paper argues that AI leaderboards are structurally ill-suited to serving the Global South because they lack independent governance, conflict-of-interest policies, and mechanisms for metric evolution. The barrier is not missing data; high-quality regional benchmarks already exist: IndicSUPERB, MILU, and LAHAJA for India; IrokoBench for Africa; AlGhafa for Arabic. The barrier is institutional design. Global leaderboards do not include these benchmarks, and no governance mechanism compels them to do so. Commercial pressure corrects leaderboard failures when paying customers in the Global North are affected. The Global South lacks equivalent leverage. Without governance, failures affecting Hindi, Swahili, or Arabic speakers persist indefinitely as documented but unaddressed gaps. Using India as a case study (1.4 billion people, 22 scheduled languages, high-quality benchmarks, but no trusted aggregation), we report findings from a consultation with 58 AI practitioners showing consistent preference for formal governance and disclosure-based conflict management. The solution is not more data but better institutions: regional leaderboards with independent governance from the start.

</details>


### [96] [Safety Alignment Illusion: The Cross-Lingual Safety Gap in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18131)
*Namya Bhatnagar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出INCLUDE，一个用于量化印度中心社会文化偏见的多语言评估基准，涵盖6种提示语言（英语、印地语、孟加拉语、马拉地语、泰米尔语和Hinglish），包含2,604条提示，评估了10个开源和闭源LLM，分析了14,988个偏见分数。实验发现两个关键结果：开源模型中孟加拉语产生最高平均偏见分数；英语在开源模型中偏见最低但在闭源模型中偏见最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的安全对齐训练高度以英语为中心，当安全过滤器对非英语语言失效时，语音助手和口语对话系统可能产生强化刻板印象的输出，绕过标准英语安全对齐，向非英语社区传播有害偏见。对于部署在印度多语言人群中的口语技术，这是一个关键失败模式。因此需要解决跨语言安全对齐差距。

Method: 作者引入了INCLUDE（Indian Cultural Lens for Understanding and Detecting Embedded Biases）多语言评估基准。该基准包含2,604条提示，覆盖六种提示语言：英语、印地语、孟加拉语、马拉地语、泰米尔语以及Hinglish（印地语-英语代码混合）。作者对10个开源和闭源LLM进行了评估，分析了14,988个偏见分数。

Result: 统计结果表明两个关键发现：第一，孟加拉语在开源模型中产生了最高的平均偏见分数；第二，英语呈现显著反转现象——在开源模型中产生最低偏见，但在闭源模型中产生最高偏见。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了LLM安全对齐在跨语言场景下的系统性漏洞，特别是面向印度多语言社区时，现有英语中心化的安全对齐策略无法有效覆盖非英语语言。研究结果强调了构建多语言、文化敏感的偏见检测基准的必要性，为未来改进多语言安全对齐提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Current safety alignment training for Large Language Models (LLMs) are heavily English-centric. When such safety filters fail for non-English languages, the consequences are immediate and user-facing: voice assistants and spoken dialogue systems may produce stereotype-reinforcing outputs, bypassing the standard English-focused safety alignments and propagating harmful bias to non-English speaking communities. For spoken language technologies deployed across India's linguistically diverse population, this represents a critical failure mode. To address this cross-lingual gap, we introduce INCLUDE (Indian Cultural Lens for Understanding and Detecting Embedded Biases), a multilingual evaluation benchmark designed to quantify Indian-centric socio-cultural biases. INCLUDE consists of 2,604 prompts spanning six prompt languages: English, Hindi, Bengali, Marathi, Tamil, and Hinglish (Hindi-English code-mix). We evaluate ten open- and closed-source LLMs against this benchmark, analyzing 14,988 bias scores. Our statistical results reveal two key findings. First, Bengali yielded the highest average bias score in open-source models. Second, English demonstrated a notable reversal, producing the lowest bias in open-source models but the highest bias in closed-source models.

</details>


### [97] [Optimized Fuzzy Logic Approach with the IEEE Key Gas Method for Diagnosing Power Transformer Faults Using Dissolved Gas Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18133)
*Kim-Anh Nguyen,Huy Hoang Le,Ba Tu Phung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将模糊逻辑与IEEE关键气体法（KGM）相结合的增强模型FL-KGM，通过改进隶属函数、优化模糊规则集以及分离CO与CO2，实现了高达98.6%的变压器故障诊断准确率，显著优于传统KGM及其他基于模糊逻辑的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变压器故障诊断对电力系统稳定性至关重要。传统的IEEE关键气体法（KGM）在油中溶解气体分析（DGA）中广泛使用，但在处理模糊数据和保证高诊断精度方面存在局限性，亟需改进。

Method: 该研究将模糊逻辑与IEEE关键气体法（KGM）相结合，提出FL-KGM模型。核心改进包括：细化隶属函数、优化模糊规则集，并创新性地将CO与CO2分离处理以消除诊断不一致性。同时采用多维气体比率分析和自适应分类框架实现故障识别与分类。

Result: 基于真实世界数据集的实验验证表明，FL-KGM模型实现了高达98.6%的诊断准确率，显著优于传统KGM方法和其他基于模糊逻辑的既有方法。

Conclusion: FL-KGM模型在推进变压器监测、实现智能故障检测和增强现代电力系统预测性维护策略方面展现出巨大潜力。

Abstract: Reliable transformer fault diagnosis is essential for maintaining power system stability. The IEEE Key Gas Method (KGM), a widely utilized approach in Dissolved Gas Analysis (DGA), exhibits limitations in addressing ambiguous data and ensuring high diagnostic accuracy. This study presents An enhanced model combining Fuzzy Logic with the IEEE Key Gas Method (FL-KGM) that introduces refined membership functions, optimized fuzzy rule sets, and a novel separation of CO and CO2 to eliminate diagnostic inconsistencies. By leveraging multidimensional gas ratio analysis and an adaptive classification framework, FL-KGM delivers superior fault identification and classification. Experimental validation utilizing real-world datasets demonstrates that FL-KGM achieves up to 98.6% accuracy, significantly outperforming KGM and other FL-based approaches. These findings elucidate the potential of FL-KGM in advancing transformer monitoring, enabling intelligent fault detection, and enhancing predictive maintenance strategies in modern power systems.

</details>


### [98] [Improving Rural Medication Safety with AI: A Scoping Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18135)
*Jeong-ah Kim,Muhammad Ashad Kabir,Daniel Terry,Maryam Rouhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文通过范围综述（2012-2025年，涵盖9个国家12项研究），系统探讨了人工智能技术在乡村医疗机构中减少用药错误和提升患者安全的应用与效果。研究发现AI已渗透至药物管理全流程（处方、配药、给药及给药后监测），可将处方和转录错误减少34%-80%，但面临着基础设施不足、人员培训缺乏、系统集成困难、预警疲劳及治理框架缺失等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用药错误是全球医疗系统的重大威胁，严重危害患者安全。乡村医疗机构因资源匮乏和专业人员短缺，用药错误风险尤为突出。引入人工智能有望改善这一现状，但目前缺乏系统性的证据整合，因此需要一项范围综述来梳理AI技术在乡村医疗环境中减少用药错误的应用现状与效果。

Method: 采用范围综述（scoping review）方法，通过系统文献检索（2012-2025年），涵盖EBSCohost、Emcare (Ovid)、MEDLINE和ProQuest Consumer Health Database多个数据库。共纳入来自9个不同国家的12项原始研究，采用主题分析法对AI干预措施在用药流程各环节的应用数据进行归纳提炼。

Result: AI技术已整合至用药管理的各阶段（处方、配药、给药和给药后监测）。提炼出四个核心主题：（1）AI类型（临床决策支持系统、机器学习、自然语言处理、智能输液泵等）；（2）受影响的用药流程阶段；（3）降低错误和提升工作流安全性的效果——机器学习监测可将处方和转录错误减少34%-80%；（4）乡村特有挑战（基础设施、员工培训、系统集成和预警疲劳）。主要障碍包括治理框架缺失、资金限制和临床医生抵触。

Conclusion: 在乡村医疗机构中，AI技术在提升用药安全方面具有巨大潜力，能够实现数据驱动的监测、流程自动化和临床决策支持。然而，需解决治理、经费和人力资源等方面的障碍，以充分发挥AI在乡村医疗环境中的价值。

Abstract: Introduction: Medication errors (MEs) represent a significant threat to global healthcare systems, contributing to patient harm. Introducing artificial intelligence (AI) in rural healthcare enhances patient safety. The aim is to explore the applications and effectiveness of AI technologies in enhancing patient safety and reducing medication errors in rural health settings.
  Methods: A scoping review was conducted through a systematic literature search spanning 2012 to 2025 across multiple databases, including EBSCohost, Emcare (Ovid), MEDLINE, and the ProQuest Consumer Health Database. Twelve primary studies from nine different nations were examined. Data were analysed thematically to obtain insights on AI interventions across the medication process.
  Results: AI technologies have been integrated into every stage of medication management, right from prescribing and dispensing to administration and post-administration monitoring. Four key themes came to light: (1) the various types of AI being utilised (like Clinical Decision Support Systems, Machine Learning, Natural Language Processing, and smart pumps); (2) the phases of the medication process that are affected; (3) how effective these technologies are in minimising errors and boosting workflow safety; and (4) rural-specific challenges including infrastructure, staff training, system integration, and alert fatigue. Several studies have demonstrated that machine learning-based surveillance improves incident detection and reduces prescribing and transcription errors by an impressive 34% to 80%. Barriers included lack of governance frameworks, financial limitations, and clinician resistance, which still present major obstacles.
  Conclusion: In rural healthcare, AI technologies hold great potential for enhancing pharmaceutical safety. They can allow data-driven monitoring, automate processes, and offer clinical decision assistance.

</details>


### [99] [FraudBench: Stress-Testing Policy-Grounded Banking Agents Against Adaptive Fraud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18136)
*Dheeraj Mohandas Pai,Lu Xian*
